from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User


# =========================================================
# COLLEGE
# =========================================================

class College(models.Model):

    name = models.CharField(
        max_length=200
    )
    slug = models.SlugField(
    max_length=220,
    unique=True,
    blank=True
)
    location = models.CharField(
        max_length=200
    )

    description = models.TextField(
        blank=True
    )

    website = models.URLField(
        blank=True
    )

    phone = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        blank=True
    )

    email = models.EmailField(
        blank=True
    )

    image = models.ImageField(
        upload_to="colleges/",
        blank=True,
        null=True
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


# =========================================================
# STUDENT PROFILE
# =========================================================

class StudentProfile(models.Model):

    user = models.OneToOneField(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="student_profile"
    )

    profile_picture = models.ImageField(
        upload_to="profile_pictures/",
        blank=True,
        null=True
    )

    mobile = models.CharField(
        max_length=11,
        blank=True,
        default=""
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} Profile"


# =========================================================
# APPLICATION
# =========================================================

class Application(models.Model):

    user_id = models.CharField(
        max_length=30
    )

    email = models.EmailField()

    mobile = models.CharField(
        max_length=11
    )

    full_name = models.CharField(
        max_length=150
    )

    password = models.CharField(
        max_length=128
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return self.full_name


# =========================================================
# ADMISSION APPLICATION
# =========================================================

class AdmissionApplication(models.Model):

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("Pending", "Pending"),
        ("Approved", "Approved"),
        ("Rejected", "Rejected"),
    ]

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # STUDENT & COLLEGE
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    student = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE
    )

    college = models.ForeignKey(
        College,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # APPLICATION STATUS
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default="Pending"
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # STUDENT INFORMATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    gender = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        blank=True
    )

    location = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True
    )

    course = models.CharField(
        max_length=150,
        blank=True
    )

    full_name = models.CharField(
        max_length=150,
        blank=True
    )

    father_name = models.CharField(
        max_length=150,
        blank=True
    )

    relationship = models.CharField(
        max_length=50,
        blank=True
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # CNIC
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    guardian_cnic = models.CharField(
        max_length=13,
        blank=True
    )

    student_cnic = models.CharField(
        max_length=13,
        blank=True
    )

    date_of_birth = models.DateField(
        blank=True,
        null=True
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # CONTACT INFORMATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    guardian_contact = models.CharField(
        max_length=11,
        blank=True
    )

    student_contact = models.CharField(
        max_length=11,
        blank=True
    )

    email = models.EmailField(
        blank=True
    )

    address = models.TextField(
        blank=True
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # ACADEMIC INFORMATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    degree = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True
    )

    passing_year = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        blank=True
    )

    board = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True
    )

    group = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True
    )

    obtained_marks = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        blank=True
    )

    total_marks = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        blank=True
    )

    board_roll_no = models.CharField(
        max_length=50,
        blank=True
    )

    previous_school = models.CharField(
        max_length=200,
        blank=True
    )

    heard_about_us = models.CharField(
        max_length=100,
        blank=True
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # CREATED DATE
    # -----------------------------------------------------

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.full_name} - {self.college.name}"


# =========================================================
# NOTIFICATION
# =========================================================

class Notification(models.Model):

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="notifications"
    )

    application = models.ForeignKey(
        AdmissionApplication,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True,
        blank=True
    )

    title = models.CharField(
        max_length=200
    )

    message = models.TextField()

    is_read = models.BooleanField(
        default=False
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - {self.title}"
    
# =========================================================
# EMAIL OTP VERIFICATION
# =========================================================

class EmailOTP(models.Model):

    email = models.EmailField(db_index=True)

    code_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    expires_at = models.DateTimeField()

    attempts = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)

    is_verified = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"OTP verification for {self.email}"

import re
import secrets
from datetime import timedelta

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, login, logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.hashers import check_password, make_password
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.db import transaction
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone

from .models import (
    AdmissionApplication,
    College,
    EmailOTP,
    Notification,
    StudentProfile,
)


# =========================================================
# HELPER: RENDER ADMISSION FORM WITH CONTEXT
# =========================================================

def render_admission_form(
    request,
    colleges=None,
    college=None,
    selected_slugs=None,
    multiple_colleges=False,
):
    """
    Shared context for the common admission form.
    Supports either one college or multiple selected colleges.
    """
    context = {
        "college": college,
        "colleges": colleges or ([college] if college else []),
        "selected_slugs": selected_slugs or [],
        "multiple_colleges": multiple_colleges,
    }

    return render(request, "admission-form.html", context)


# =========================================================
# HELPER: VALIDATE ADMISSION FIELDS
# =========================================================

def validate_admission_form(request):
    guardian_cnic = request.POST.get("guardian_cnic", "").strip()
    student_cnic = request.POST.get("student_cnic", "").strip()
    guardian_contact = request.POST.get("guardian_contact", "").strip()
    student_contact = request.POST.get("student_contact", "").strip()

    if guardian_cnic and not re.fullmatch(r"\d{13}", guardian_cnic):
        return "Guardian CNIC must contain exactly 13 digits."

    if student_cnic and not re.fullmatch(r"\d{13}", student_cnic):
        return "Student CNIC must contain exactly 13 digits."

    if guardian_contact and not re.fullmatch(r"\d{11}", guardian_contact):
        return "Guardian contact number must contain exactly 11 digits."

    if student_contact and not re.fullmatch(r"\d{11}", student_contact):
        return "Student contact number must contain exactly 11 digits."

    return None


# =========================================================
# HELPER: GET ADMISSION DATA FROM FORM
# =========================================================

def get_admission_data(request):
    """
    Prepare the common fields shared by admission applications.
    """
    return {
        "gender": request.POST.get("gender", "").strip(),
        "location": request.POST.get("location", "").strip(),
        "course": request.POST.get("course", "").strip(),
        "full_name": request.POST.get("full_name", "").strip(),
        "father_name": request.POST.get("father_name", "").strip(),
        "relationship": request.POST.get("relationship", "").strip(),
        "guardian_cnic": request.POST.get("guardian_cnic", "").strip(),
        "student_cnic": request.POST.get("student_cnic", "").strip(),
        "date_of_birth": request.POST.get("date_of_birth") or None,
        "guardian_contact": request.POST.get("guardian_contact", "").strip(),
        "student_contact": request.POST.get("student_contact", "").strip(),
        "email": request.POST.get("email", "").strip(),
        "address": request.POST.get("address", "").strip(),
        "degree": request.POST.get("degree", "").strip(),
        "passing_year": request.POST.get("passing_year", "").strip(),
        "board": request.POST.get("board", "").strip(),
        "group": request.POST.get("group", "").strip(),
        "obtained_marks": request.POST.get("obtained_marks", "").strip(),
        "total_marks": request.POST.get("total_marks", "").strip(),
        "board_roll_no": request.POST.get("board_roll_no", "").strip(),
        "previous_school": request.POST.get("previous_school", "").strip(),
        "heard_about_us": request.POST.get("heard_about_us", "").strip(),
    }


# =========================================================
# HELPER: CREATE APPLICATIONS FOR SELECTED COLLEGES
# =========================================================

def create_applications_for_colleges(request, colleges, application_data):
    """
    Create applications using the same fields for one or more colleges.
    Atomic transaction prevents a partial set of applications.
    """
    with transaction.atomic():
        for college in colleges:
            AdmissionApplication.objects.create(
                student=request.user,
                college=college,
                **application_data,
            )


# =========================================================
# MAIN WEBSITE
# =========================================================

def home(request):
    colleges = College.objects.all().order_by("name")

    return render(
        request,
        "index.html",
        {"colleges": colleges},
    )


def college_detail(request, id):
    college = get_object_or_404(College, id=id)

    return render(
        request,
        "college_detail.html",
        {"college": college},
    )

# =========================================================
# CHOOSE COLLEGE

@login_required
def choose_college(request):
    colleges = College.objects.all().order_by("name")

    return render(
        request,
        "choose-college.html",
        {"colleges": colleges},
    )


# =========================================================
# SIGN UP / LOGIN
# =========================================================

def application_form(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect("dashboard")

    show_login = request.GET.get("mode") == "login"

    if request.method == "POST":
        form_type = request.POST.get("form_type", "signup")
        email = request.POST.get("email", "").strip().lower()
        password = request.POST.get("password", "")

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # LOGIN
        # -------------------------------------------------
        if form_type == "login":
            show_login = True

            if not email or not password:
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Please enter your email and password.",
                )
            else:
                users = User.objects.filter(email__iexact=email)

                if users.count() != 1:
                    messages.error(
                        request,
                        "Invalid email or password.",
                    )
                else:
                    matching_user = users.first()

                    user = authenticate(
                        request,
                        username=matching_user.username,
                        password=password,
                    )

                    if user is None:
                        if not matching_user.is_active:
                            messages.error(
                                request,
                                "Please verify your email before logging in.",
                            )
                        else:
                            messages.error(
                                request,
                                "Invalid email or password.",
                            )
                    else:
                        login(request, user)
                        return redirect("choose-college")

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # SIGNUP
        # -------------------------------------------------
        elif form_type == "signup":
            full_name = request.POST.get("full_name", "").strip()
            mobile = request.POST.get("mobile", "").strip()
            confirm_password = request.POST.get("confirm_password", "")

            if not all([
                full_name,
                email,
                mobile,
                password,
                confirm_password,
            ]):
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Please complete all required fields.",
                )

            elif not re.fullmatch(r"\d{11}", mobile):
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Mobile number must contain exactly 11 digits.",
                )

            elif password != confirm_password:
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Passwords do not match.",
                )

            else:
                try:
                    validate_password(password)
                except ValidationError as error:
                    for message in error.messages:
                        messages.error(request, message)
                else:
                    existing_user = User.objects.filter(
                        email__iexact=email
                    ).first()

                    if existing_user and existing_user.is_active:
                        messages.error(
                            request,
                            "This email already has an account. Please log in.",
                        )
                    else:
                        try:
                            with transaction.atomic():
                                if existing_user is None:
                                    user = User.objects.create_user(
                                        username=email,
                                        email=email,
                                        first_name=full_name,
                                        password=password,
                                        is_active=False,
                                    )
                                else:
                                    user = existing_user
                                    user.email = email
                                    user.first_name = full_name
                                    user.set_password(password)
                                    user.save(
                                        update_fields=[
                                            "email",
                                            "first_name",
                                            "password",
                                        ]
                                    )

                                request.session[
                                    "pending_verification_email"
                                ] = email

                                request.session[
                                    "pending_verification_mobile"
                                ] = mobile

                                generate_email_otp(email)

                            messages.success(
                                request,
                                "A verification code has been sent to your email. "
                                "Please check your inbox.",
                            )
                            return redirect("verify-email")

                        except Exception:
                            request.session.pop(
                                "pending_verification_email", None
                            )
                            request.session.pop(
                                "pending_verification_mobile", None
                            )
                            messages.error(
                                request,
                                "We could not send the verification code. "
                                "Please try again.",
                            )

        else:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Invalid form submission.",
            )

    return render(
        request,
        "application-form.html",
        {"show_login": show_login},
    )


# =========================================================
# EMAIL VERIFICATION
# =========================================================

def verify_email(request):
    email = request.session.get("pending_verification_email")
    mobile = request.session.get("pending_verification_mobile")

    if not email:
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Please sign up first to verify your email.",
        )
        return redirect("application-form")

    if request.method == "POST":
        code = request.POST.get("otp", "").strip()

        otp_record = EmailOTP.objects.filter(
            email__iexact=email,
            is_verified=False,
        ).order_by("-created_at").first()

        if otp_record is None:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "No active verification code found. Please sign up again.",
            )

        elif timezone.now() >= otp_record.expires_at:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Your verification code has expired. Please request a new one.",
            )

        elif otp_record.attempts >= 5:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Too many incorrect attempts. Please request a new code.",
            )

        elif not check_password(code, otp_record.code_hash):
            otp_record.attempts += 1
            otp_record.save(update_fields=["attempts"])

            messages.error(
                request,
                "Incorrect verification code.",
            )

        else:
            with transaction.atomic():
                user = User.objects.select_for_update().filter(
                    email__iexact=email,
                    is_active=False,
                ).first()

                if user is None:
                    messages.error(
                        request,
                        "Account not found or already verified. Please log in.",
                    )
                    return redirect("application-form")

                profile, _ = StudentProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=user)

                if mobile and re.fullmatch(r"\d{11}", mobile):
                    profile.mobile = mobile
                    profile.save(update_fields=["mobile"])

                user.is_active = True
                user.save(update_fields=["is_active"])

                otp_record.is_verified = True
                otp_record.save(update_fields=["is_verified"])

            request.session.pop("pending_verification_email", None)
            request.session.pop("pending_verification_mobile", None)

            login(request, user)

            messages.success(
                request,
                "Email verified successfully!",
            )
            return redirect("choose-college")

    return render(
        request,
        "verify-email.html",
        {"email": email},
    )


# =========================================================
# RESEND EMAIL OTP
# =========================================================

def generate_email_otp(email):
    email = email.strip().lower()
    otp = f"{secrets.randbelow(1_000_000):06d}"
    expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=5)

    sent = send_mail(
        subject="Your College Portal Verification Code",
        message=(
            f"Your email verification code is {otp}.\n\n"
            "This code expires in 5 minutes. "
            "If you did not request it, ignore this email."
        ),
        from_email=None,
        recipient_list=[email],
        fail_silently=False,
    )

    if sent != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("Verification email could not be sent.")

    EmailOTP.objects.filter(
        email__iexact=email,
        is_verified=False,
    ).delete()

    EmailOTP.objects.create(
        email=email,
        code_hash=make_password(otp),
        expires_at=expires_at,
    )


def resend_email_otp(request):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("verify-email")

    email = request.session.get("pending_verification_email")

    if not email:
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Please sign up first to verify your email.",
        )
        return redirect("application-form")

    latest_otp = EmailOTP.objects.filter(
        email__iexact=email,
        is_verified=False,
    ).order_by("-created_at").first()

    if latest_otp is not None:
        seconds_since_sent = (
            timezone.now() - latest_otp.created_at
        ).total_seconds()

        if seconds_since_sent < 60:
            wait_seconds = max(1, int(60 - seconds_since_sent))
            messages.error(
                request,
                f"Please wait {wait_seconds} seconds before requesting another code.",
            )
            return redirect("verify-email")

    try:
        generate_email_otp(email)
    except Exception:
        messages.error(
            request,
            "We could not send a new code. Please try again later.",
        )
    else:
        messages.success(
            request,
            "A new verification code has been sent to your email. "
            "The previous code is no longer valid.",
        )

    return redirect("verify-email")


# =========================================================
# STUDENT DASHBOARD
# =========================================================

@login_required
def dashboard(request):
    applications = AdmissionApplication.objects.filter(
        student=request.user
    ).order_by("-created_at")

    unread_notifications = Notification.objects.filter(
        user=request.user,
        is_read=False,
    )

    context = {
        "applications": applications,
        "total_applications": applications.count(),
        "pending_applications": applications.filter(status="Pending").count(),
        "approved_applications": applications.filter(status="Approved").count(),
        "rejected_applications": applications.filter(status="Rejected").count(),
        "unread_notifications_count": unread_notifications.count(),
        "latest_notification": unread_notifications.order_by(
            "-created_at"
        ).first(),
    }

    return render(request, "dashboard.html", context)

# NOTIFICATIONS
# =========================================================

@login_required
def notifications(request):
    notifications_list = Notification.objects.filter(
        user=request.user
    ).order_by("-created_at")

    return render(
        request,
        "notifications.html",
        {"notifications": notifications_list},
    )


@login_required
def mark_notification_read(request, id):
    notification = get_object_or_404(
        Notification,
        id=id,
        user=request.user,
    )

    notification.is_read = True
    notification.save(update_fields=["is_read"])

    if notification.application:
        return redirect(
            "application-detail",
            id=notification.application.id,
        )

    return redirect("notifications")


@login_required
def mark_all_notifications_read(request):
    Notification.objects.filter(
        user=request.user,
        is_read=False,
    ).update(is_read=True)

    return redirect("notifications")


# =========================================================
# STUDENT PROFILE
# =========================================================

@login_required
def profile(request):
    student_profile, _ = StudentProfile.objects.get_or_create(
        user=request.user
    )

    if request.method == "POST":
        action = request.POST.get("action")

        if action == "upload_picture":
            picture = request.FILES.get("profile_picture")

            if picture:
                student_profile.profile_picture = picture
                student_profile.save()

                messages.success(
                    request,
                    "Profile picture updated successfully.",
                )
            else:
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Please select a picture first.",
                )

            return redirect("profile")

        if action == "remove_picture":
            if student_profile.profile_picture:
                student_profile.profile_picture.delete(save=False)
                student_profile.profile_picture = None
                student_profile.save()

                messages.success(
                    request,
                    "Profile picture removed successfully.",
                )
            else:
                messages.info(
                    request,
                    "You do not have a profile picture.",
                )

            return redirect("profile")

    applications_count = AdmissionApplication.objects.filter(
        student=request.user
    ).count()

    return render(
        request,
        "profile.html",
        {
            "applications_count": applications_count,
            "student_profile": student_profile,
        },
    )

# =========================================================
# COMMON ADMISSION APPLICATION â€” SINGLE COLLEGE
# =========================================================

@login_required
def admission_application_common(request, slug):
    college = get_object_or_404(College, slug=slug)

    if request.method == "POST":
        validation_error = validate_admission_form(request)

        if validation_error:
            messages.error(request, validation_error)
            return render_admission_form(request, college=college)

        application_data = get_admission_data(request)

        try:
            create_applications_for_colleges(
                request,
                [college],
                application_data,
            )
        except (ValidationError, ValueError, TypeError):
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Some application information is invalid. Please review the form.",
            )
            return render_admission_form(request, college=college)

        messages.success(
            request,
            f"Your admission application for {college.name} "
            "has been submitted successfully!",
        )
        return redirect("dashboard")

    return render_admission_form(request, college=college)

@login_required
def admission_application(request):
    """
    Preserve the existing admission-application URL.
    The college is looked up dynamically by its existing database name.
    """
    college = get_object_or_404(
        College,
        name="Punjab College Chishtian",
    )

    if request.method == "POST":
        validation_error = validate_admission_form(request)

        if validation_error:
            messages.error(request, validation_error)
            return render(request, "applypgc.html", {"college": college})

        try:
            create_applications_for_colleges(
                request,
                [college],
                get_admission_data(request),
            )
        except (ValidationError, ValueError, TypeError):
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Some application information is invalid. Please review the form.",
            )
            return render(request, "applypgc.html", {"college": college})

        messages.success(
            request,
            "Your admission application has been submitted successfully!",
        )
        return redirect("dashboard")

    return render(request, "applypgc.html", {"college": college})


# =========================================================
# COMMON ADMISSION APPLICATION â€” MULTIPLE COLLEGES
# =========================================================

@login_required
def admission_application_multiple(request):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("choose-college")

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # STAGE 1: COLLEGE SELECTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------
    if request.POST.get("submission_stage") != "admission_form":
        selected_slugs = list(dict.fromkeys(
            request.POST.getlist("college_slugs")
        ))

        if not selected_slugs:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Please select at least one college.",
            )
            return redirect("choose-college")

        colleges_by_slug = {
            college.slug: college
            for college in College.objects.filter(
                slug__in=selected_slugs
            )
        }

        if (
            len(colleges_by_slug) != len(selected_slugs)
            or any(not slug for slug in selected_slugs)
        ):
            messages.error(
                request,
                "One or more selected colleges are invalid.",
            )
            return redirect("choose-college")

        colleges = [colleges_by_slug[slug] for slug in selected_slugs]

        return render_admission_form(
            request,
            colleges=colleges,
            selected_slugs=selected_slugs,
            multiple_colleges=True,
        )

    # -----------------------------------------------------
    # STAGE 2: FINAL FORM SUBMISSION
    # -----------------------------------------------------
    selected_slugs = list(dict.fromkeys(
        request.POST.getlist("selected_college_slugs")
    ))

    if not selected_slugs or any(not slug for slug in selected_slugs):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "No valid colleges were selected. Please start again.",
        )
        return redirect("choose-college")

    colleges_by_slug = {
        college.slug: college
        for college in College.objects.filter(
            slug__in=selected_slugs
        )
    }

    if len(colleges_by_slug) != len(selected_slugs):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "One or more selected colleges are invalid.",
        )
        return redirect("choose-college")

    colleges = [colleges_by_slug[slug] for slug in selected_slugs]

    # These fields were required by the existing workflow.
    required_fields = [
        "gender",
        "location",
        "course",
        "full_name",
        "father_name",
        "relationship",
        "guardian_cnic",
        "student_cnic",
        "date_of_birth",
        "guardian_contact",
        "email",
        "degree",
        "passing_year",
        "board",
        "group",
        "obtained_marks",
        "total_marks",
        "board_roll_no",
        "previous_school",
        "heard_about_us",
    ]

    missing_fields = [
        field
        for field in required_fields
        if not request.POST.get(field, "").strip()
    ]

    if missing_fields:
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Please complete all required fields.",
        )
        return render_admission_form(
            request,
            colleges=colleges,
            selected_slugs=selected_slugs,
            multiple_colleges=True,
        )

    # Accept both common checkbox values: "on" and "yes".
    if request.POST.get("confirm_terms") not in {"on", "yes", "true", "1"}:
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Please accept the terms and conditions.",
        )
        return render_admission_form(
            request,
            colleges=colleges,
            selected_slugs=selected_slugs,
            multiple_colleges=True,
        )

    validation_error = validate_admission_form(request)

    if validation_error:
        messages.error(request, validation_error)
        return render_admission_form(
            request,
            colleges=colleges,
            selected_slugs=selected_slugs,
            multiple_colleges=True,
        )

    application_data = get_admission_data(request)

    try:
        create_applications_for_colleges(
            request,
            colleges,
            application_data,
        )
    except (ValidationError, ValueError, TypeError):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Some application information is invalid. Please review the form.",
        )
        return render_admission_form(
            request,
            colleges=colleges,
            selected_slugs=selected_slugs,
            multiple_colleges=True,
        )

    messages.success(
        request,
        f"Applications submitted successfully to {len(colleges)} college(s).",
    )
    return redirect("dashboard")

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'config.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

/* =====================================================
   SUPERIOR COLLEGE â€” SHARED RESPONSIVE STYLESHEET
   Used by all Superior College pages
   ===================================================== */

/* 1. GLOBAL VARIABLES */
:root {
    --superior-primary: #163c32;
    --superior-secondary: #0b2923;
    --superior-accent: #d7b56d;
    --superior-text: #222222;

FILE: static\js\csc-common.js

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("csc-menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.getElementById("csc-navbar");
    const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

    if (menuToggle && navbar) {
        menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
        });

        document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown-toggle").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                    event.preventDefault();

                    const dropdown = this.closest(".csc-dropdown");
                    if (!dropdown) return;

                    document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                        if (item !== dropdown) item.classList.remove("open");
                    });

                    dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
                }
            });
        });

        navbar.querySelectorAll("a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (
                    window.innerWidth <= 767 &&
                    !this.classList.contains("csc-dropdown-toggle")
                ) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("active");
                    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                }
            });
        });

        window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
                navbar.classList.remove("active");
                menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    item.classList.remove("open");
                });
            }
        });
    }

    if (backToTop) {
        window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
            backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "block" : "none";
        });

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        });
    }

    // Undergraduate page accordion
    document.querySelectorAll(".degree-header").forEach(function (header) {
        header.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const item = this.closest(".degree-item");
            if (!item) return;

            item.classList.toggle("open");
            this.setAttribute(
                "aria-expanded",
                String(item.classList.contains("open"))
            );
        });
    });
});


============================================================
FILE: static\js\pgc-common.js
============================================================

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const toggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");
    const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

    if (toggle && navbar) {
        toggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const opened = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
            toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(opened));

            const icon = toggle.querySelector("i");
            if (icon) {
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !opened);
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-xmark", opened);
            }
        });

        navbar.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                if (window.innerWidth > 768) return;

                event.preventDefault();
                const submenu = link.nextElementSibling;
                if (!submenu || !submenu.classList.contains("sub-menu")) return;

                const open = submenu.style.display !== "block";

                navbar.querySelectorAll(".sub-menu").forEach(function (other) {
                    other.style.display = "none";
                });

                submenu.style.display = open ? "block" : "none";
            });
        });

        navbar.querySelectorAll(":scope > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                navbar.classList.remove("active");
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            });
        });

        document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (
                window.innerWidth <= 768 &&
                !navbar.contains(event.target) &&
                !toggle.contains(event.target)
            ) {
                navbar.classList.remove("active");
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            }
        });

        window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
                navbar.classList.remove("active");
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                navbar.querySelectorAll(".sub-menu").forEach(function (submenu) {
                    submenu.style.display = "";
                });
            }
        });
    }

    function updateBackToTop() {
        if (backToTop) {
            backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 250 ? "flex" : "none";
        }
    }

    if (backToTop) {
        updateBackToTop();
        window.addEventListener("scroll", updateBackToTop);

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        });
    }
});


============================================================
FILE: static\js\superior-common.js
============================================================

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");
    const dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll(".navbar .dropdown");
    const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

    // Mobile navigation menu
    if (menuToggle && navbar) {
        menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("show");

            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
            menuToggle.setAttribute(
                "aria-label",
                isOpen ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu"
            );
            menuToggle.textContent = isOpen ? "âœ•" : "â˜°";
        });
    }

    // Mobile dropdown menus
    dropdowns.forEach(function (dropdown) {
        const button = dropdown.querySelector(".dropdown-toggle");

        if (!button) return;

        button.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = dropdown.classList.contains("active");

            dropdowns.forEach(function (otherDropdown) {
                otherDropdown.classList.remove("active");

                const otherButton =
                    otherDropdown.querySelector(".dropdown-toggle");

                if (otherButton) {
                    otherButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                }
            });

            if (!isOpen) {
                dropdown.classList.add("active");
                button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
            } else {
                button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            }
        });
    });

    // Back to top button
    if (backToTop) {
        window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
            backToTop.classList.toggle("visible", window.scrollY > 300);
        });

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
            window.scrollTo({
                top: 0,
                behavior: "smooth"
            });
        });
    }
});

FILE: static\notifications.js

const enableBrowserNotifications = () => {
if (!("Notification" in window)) {
return;
}

if (Notification.permission === "default") {
    Notification.requestPermission();
}

};

const showBrowserNotification = (title, message) => {
if (!("Notification" in window)) {
return;
}

if (Notification.permission === "granted") {
    new Notification(title, {
        body: message,
        icon: "/static/images/logo.png"
    });
}

};

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
enableBrowserNotifications();

const notificationData = document.getElementById("notification-data");

if (notificationData) {
    const title = notificationData.dataset.title;
    const message = notificationData.dataset.message;

    if (title && message) {
        showBrowserNotification(title, message);
    }
}

});

FILE: static\riphah-common.js
============================================================
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const menuButton = document.querySelector(".riphah-menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.querySelector(".riphah-navbar");
    const dropdownToggles = document.querySelectorAll(".riphah-dropdown-toggle");
    const backToTop = document.getElementById("riphah-back-to-top");

    // Mobile navigation menu
    if (menuButton && navbar) {
        menuButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("riphah-active");

            menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
            menuButton.setAttribute(
                "aria-label",
                isOpen ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu"
            );

            const icon = menuButton.querySelector("i");
            if (icon) {
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !isOpen);
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-times", isOpen);
            }
        });
    }

    // Mobile dropdown menus
    dropdownToggles.forEach(function (toggle) {
        toggle.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches) {
                event.preventDefault();

                const dropdown = toggle.closest(".riphah-dropdown");
                if (!dropdown) return;

                const isOpen = dropdown.classList.toggle("riphah-open");
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
            }
        });
    });

    // Close mobile menu when a regular navigation link is selected
    if (navbar && menuButton) {
        navbar.querySelectorAll("a:not(.riphah-dropdown-toggle)").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("riphah-active");
                    menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                    const icon = menuButton.querySelector("i");
                    if (icon) {
                        icon.classList.add("fa-bars");
                        icon.classList.remove("fa-times");
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    }

    // Back-to-top button
    if (backToTop) {
        function updateBackToTop() {
            backToTop.classList.toggle(
                "riphah-back-to-top-visible",
                window.scrollY > 300
            );
        }

        window.addEventListener("scroll", updateBackToTop, { passive: true });
        updateBackToTop();

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        });
    }
});

    <p class="subtitle">Login to continue your college application.</p>

   <form method="POST" action="{% url 'application-form' %}">
    {% csrf_token %}
    <input type="hidden" name="form_type" value="login">

        <div class="field">
            <label for="login-email">Email Address</label>
            <input id="login-email" type="email" name="email"
                   placeholder="Enter your email" autocomplete="email" required>
        </div>

        <div class="field">
            <label for="login-password">Password</label>
            <div class="password-wrap">
                <input id="login-password" type="password" name="password"
                       placeholder="Enter your password"
                       autocomplete="current-password" required>
                <button class="toggle-password" type="button"
                        aria-label="Show password"
                        aria-pressed="false"
                        onclick="togglePassword('login-password', this)">
                    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
                         stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"
                         aria-hidden="true">
                        <path d="M2 12s3.5-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.5 7-10 7S2 12 2 12z"/>
                        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>
                    </svg>
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>

        <button class="submit-btn" type="submit">Sign In</button>
    </form>

    <div class="link">
        Don't have an account?
        <a href="#signup" onclick="showSignup(); return false;">Create Account</a>
    </div>
</div>


<script>
    function showLogin() {
        document.getElementById("signup").classList.add("hidden");
        document.getElementById("login").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function showSignup() {
        document.getElementById("login").classList.add("hidden");
        document.getElementById("signup").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function togglePassword(inputId, button) {
        const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
        const showing = input.type === "password";

        input.type = showing ? "text" : "password";
        button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(showing));
        button.setAttribute(
            "aria-label",
            showing ? "Hide password" : "Show password"
        );
    }
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>

    document.getElementById("superiorApplyForm").addEventListener("submit", function(event) {

        const guardianCNIC = document.querySelector('input[name="guardian_cnic"]').value.trim();
        const studentCNIC = document.querySelector('input[name="student_cnic"]').value.trim();
        const guardianContact = document.querySelector('input[name="guardian_contact"]').value.trim();
        const studentContact = document.querySelector('input[name="student_contact"]').value.trim();

        if (guardianCNIC !== "" && !/^[0-9]{13}$/.test(guardianCNIC)) {

            event.preventDefault();

            alert("Guardian CNIC must contain exactly 13 digits.");

            return;
        }

        if (studentCNIC !== "" && !/^[0-9]{13}$/.test(studentCNIC)) {

            event.preventDefault();

            alert("Student CNIC / B-Form must contain exactly 13 digits.");

            return;
        }

        if (!/^[0-9]{11}$/.test(guardianContact)) {

            event.preventDefault();

            alert("Guardian Contact must contain exactly 11 digits.");

            return;
        }

        if (studentContact !== "" && !/^[0-9]{11}$/.test(studentContact)) {

            event.preventDefault();

            alert("Student Contact must contain exactly 11 digits if provided.");

            return;
        }

    });

</script>

<script>
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

    menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        navbar.classList.toggle("active");
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown-toggle").forEach(function (toggle) {
        toggle.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                event.preventDefault();
                const dropdown = this.parentElement;

                document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    if (item !== dropdown) item.classList.remove("open");
                });

                dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
            }
        });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".navbar a:not(.dropdown-toggle)").forEach(function (link) {
        link.addEventListener("click", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 768) navbar.classList.remove("active");
        });
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
            navbar.classList.remove("active");
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });
        }
    });

    document.getElementById("moreBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
        document.getElementById("moreBlogs").classList.add("show");
        this.style.display = "none";
    });

    const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

    window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
        backToTop.style.display =
            (document.body.scrollTop > 200 || document.documentElement.scrollTop > 200)
            ? "block" : "none";
    });

    backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
        const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

        // Toggle the mobile navigation.
        menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("show");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
        });

        // Open and close mobile dropdowns.
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    event.preventDefault();

                    const dropdown = this.parentElement;
                    const isOpen = dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
                    this.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
                }
            });
        });

        // Close the mobile menu after choosing a normal navigation link.
        document.querySelectorAll(".navbar > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("show");
                    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                }
            });
        });

        // Reset mobile navigation state when returning to desktop width.
        window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
                navbar.classList.remove("show");
                menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    item.classList.remove("open");
                });
            }
        });

        // Back-to-top button.
        const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

        window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
            backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
        });

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            window.scrollTo({
                top: 0,
                behavior: "smooth"
            });
        });
    </script>

</body>
</html>

<script>

/* ================= AUTO IMAGE SLIDER ================= */

let slides = document.querySelectorAll(".slide");
let slideIndex = 0;

function showSlides(){

    slides.forEach(function(slide){
        slide.classList.remove("active");
    });

    slides[slideIndex].classList.add("active");

    slideIndex++;

    if(slideIndex >= slides.length){
        slideIndex = 0;
    }

}

setInterval(showSlides,3000);


/* ================= REVEAL ANIMATION ================= */

function revealElements(){

    const reveals = document.querySelectorAll(".reveal");

    reveals.forEach(function(element){

        const windowHeight = window.innerHeight;
        const elementTop = element.getBoundingClientRect().top;
        const visible = 150;

        if(elementTop < windowHeight - visible){
            element.classList.add("active");
        }

    });

}

window.addEventListener("scroll",revealElements);

window.addEventListener("load",revealElements);


/* ================= STATS COUNTERS ================= */

const counters = document.querySelectorAll(".counter");

let counterStarted = false;

function startCounters(){

    if(counterStarted){
        return;
    }

    const statsSection = document.querySelector(".stats-section");

    if(!statsSection){
        return;
    }

    const sectionTop = statsSection.getBoundingClientRect().top;

    if(sectionTop < window.innerHeight - 100){

        counterStarted = true;

        counters.forEach(function(counter){

            counter.innerText = "0";

            const target = Number(counter.getAttribute("data-target"));

            const duration = 1800;
            const startTime = performance.now();

            function updateCounter(currentTime){

                const elapsed = currentTime - startTime;
                const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration,1);

                const currentValue = Math.floor(progress * target);

                counter.innerText = currentValue;

                if(progress < 1){
                    requestAnimationFrame(updateCounter);
                }else{
                    counter.innerText = target;
                }

            }

            requestAnimationFrame(updateCounter);

        });

    }

}

window.addEventListener("scroll",startCounters);
window.addEventListener("load",startCounters);


/* ================= SHOW MORE EVENTS ================= */

const moreBtn = document.getElementById("moreBtn");
const hiddenEvents = document.querySelectorAll(".hidden");

moreBtn.addEventListener("click",function(){

    hiddenEvents.forEach(function(event){

        event.style.display = "block";

    });

    moreBtn.style.display = "none";

});

</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    const form = document.getElementById("collegeSelectionForm");

    if (form) {
        form.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            const checked = form.querySelectorAll(
                'input[name="college_slugs"]:checked'
            );

            const error = document.getElementById("selectionError");

            if (checked.length === 0) {
                event.preventDefault();
                error.style.display = "block";
            } else {
                error.style.display = "none";
            }
        });
    }
</script>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    /*
      Front-end demonstration only.
      This does not send an email or save the form to the database.
    */
    const contactForm = document.getElementById("contactForm");
    const successMsg = document.getElementById("successMsg");

    if (contactForm && successMsg) {
        contactForm.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            if (!contactForm.checkValidity()) {
                contactForm.reportValidity();
                return;
            }

            successMsg.hidden = false;
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const form = document.getElementById("contactForm");
    const message = document.getElementById("successMsg");

    if (form && message) {
        form.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            if (!form.reportValidity()) {
                return;
            }

            message.style.display = "block";
            message.scrollIntoView({
                behavior: "smooth",
                block: "center"
            });
        });
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

    <script>
        const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
        const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

        // Mobile navigation.
        menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("show");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
        });

        // Mobile dropdowns.
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    event.preventDefault();

                    const dropdown = this.parentElement;
                    const isOpen = dropdown.classList.toggle("open");

                    this.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
                }
            });
        });

        // Close mobile navigation after selecting a normal link.
        document.querySelectorAll(".navbar > a").forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("show");
                    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                }
            });
        });

        // Reset mobile navigation when switching to desktop.
        window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
                navbar.classList.remove("show");
                menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    item.classList.remove("open");
                });
            }
        });

        // Demo-only form message.
        // This does not send or save the message to Django.
        const contactForm = document.getElementById("contactForm");
        const successMsg = document.getElementById("successMsg");

        contactForm.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            if (!contactForm.reportValidity()) {
                return;
            }

            successMsg.style.display = "block";
        });

        // Back to top.
        const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

        window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
            backToTop.style.display =
                window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
        });

        backToTop.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            window.scrollTo({
                top: 0,
                behavior: "smooth"
            });
        });
    </script>

</body>
</html>

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const form = document.getElementById("contactRipForm");

    if (!form) return;

    form.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
        if (!form.checkValidity()) {

            form.reportValidity();

    });
});

<script>
(function () {
    const button = document.getElementById("csc-back-to-top");
    if (!button) return;

    function updateButton() {
        button.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
    }

    window.addEventListener("scroll", updateButton);
    window.addEventListener("load", updateButton);

    button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    });

    updateButton();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    const menu = document.getElementById("csc-navbar");
    const toggle = document.getElementById("csc-menu-toggle");

    if (!menu || !toggle) return;

    toggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const isOpen = menu.classList.toggle("active");
        toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));

        const icon = toggle.querySelector("i");
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !isOpen);
            icon.classList.toggle("fa-xmark", isOpen);
        }
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown-toggle").forEach(function (link) {
        link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                event.preventDefault();
                const dropdown = link.closest(".csc-dropdown");

                document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    if (item !== dropdown) item.classList.remove("open");
                });

                dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
            }
        });
    });

    menu.querySelectorAll("a:not(.csc-dropdown-toggle)").forEach(function (link) {
        link.addEventListener("click", function () {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                menu.classList.remove("active");
                document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    item.classList.remove("open");
                });
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                const icon = toggle.querySelector("i");
                if (icon) {
                    icon.classList.remove("fa-xmark");
                    icon.classList.add("fa-bars");
                }
            }
        });
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
            menu.classList.remove("active");
            document.querySelectorAll(".csc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });
            toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

            const icon = toggle.querySelector("i");
            if (icon) {
                icon.classList.remove("fa-xmark");
                icon.classList.add("fa-bars");
            }
        }
    });
})();
</script>

<div class="fee-page">

    <section class="fee-hero">
        <div class="fee-orange-strip">
            <h1>Scholarship &amp;<br>Fee Concession</h1>
        </div>

        <div class="fee-image-box">
            <img src="{% static 'images/fee.jpg' %}"
                 alt="Scholarship and fee concession">
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class="fee-about">
        <p class="fee-about-text">
            Weâ€™re here to support your future success and make a
            world-class education within reach. We are committed to
            making an CSC experience accessible. Our goal is to work
            with each family to create a financial plan that provides
            the means to help you launch the career of your dreams.
        </p>
    </section>

    <section class="fee-scholarship-section">
        <h1 class="fee-title">SCHOLARSHIPS &amp; FEE CONCESSIONS</h1>

        <p class="fee-description">
            At Punjab College we believe that hard working students
            deserve rewards, and that every child deserves an education.
            Incentives and scholarships have always been a source of
            encouragement and much needed support for students. These
            awards inspire talented students to work harder and attain
            the best results in their examinations. Position holders
            are the pride of their alma mater. Punjab College offers a
            number of incentives for all those students who perform
            exceptionally well in their Boards/University exams. Other
            than incentives on the basis of performance, Punjab Group
            of Colleges is also concerned about the need of education
            of those students who canâ€™t afford the fees and provide
            financial support by giving them concession or free
            education through different categories.
        </p>

        <h2 class="fee-procedure">PROCEDURE</h2>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">1. MERIT-BASED SCHOLARSHIP:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Chishtian Science College promotes hard work and excellence.
            We offer an exclusive scholarship to students who show
            excellent performance in academics. They can avail up to a
            100% fee waiver. Students with 75% or above marks are
            eligible to apply for free education at the time of admission.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">2. TEACHERSâ€™ CHILDREN:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Chishtian Science College facilitate teachers and ensure
            that their children can avail high-quality education.
            Children of teachers at public sector and well-known private
            institutes are eligible for this concession.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">3. KINSHIP:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Chishtian Science College take care of the family of its
            students. The direct family members of old &amp; current
            students are offered a special discount on their education.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">4. ORPHAN STUDENTS:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Orphans are entitled to a special scholarship at Chishtian
            Science College. Those who have lost their fathers/guardians
            can apply under this category.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">5. STUDENTS WITH SPECIAL NEEDS:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            To motivate students with special needs to pursue education
            and nurture their minds with hope, a special fee concession
            opportunity has been ensured for them.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">6. NEED-BASED CONCESSION:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Recognizing that not all students can afford the fees and
            may face economic crises, CSC is concerned about their need
            for education and provides financial support through
            concessions or free education.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">7. 1122 EMPLOYEES:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            The employees and paramedical staff of Rescue 1122 risk
            their lives in the line of duty. They deserve our support,
            and Chishtian Science College provides special concessions
            for the children of Rescue 1122 employees.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">8. SHUHDAâ€™S CHILDREN:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Chishtian Science College takes special care of children of
            Shuhada of the armed forces and police who sacrificed their
            lives for our country. A specific concession is offered to
            help these children study without financial burden.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">9. INCENTIVE POLICIES FOR HIGH ACHIEVERS:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Chishtian Science College honours students who perform
            exceptionally well in their respective Boards. The top
            position holders in intermediate from all boards are
            awarded with a brand new car/cash prizes.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">10. INCENTIVE POLICIES FOR MATRIC TOPPERS:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Students securing top positions in matric from all boards
            are awarded with a brand new car/cash prizes upon joining
            Chishtian Science College.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">11. INCENTIVE POLICY FOR CSC STUDENTS IN UNIVERSITIES:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            Old students of CSC can avail concessions at the time of
            admission at the University of Central Punjab-Lahore,
            Mohammad Ali Jinnah University-Karachi, and Capital
            University of Science &amp; Technology-Islamabad.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">12. SPECIAL CIRCUMSTANCES FINANCIAL AID:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            If the father/guardian of any current student unfortunately
            passes away, the responsibility of their fees will be taken
            over by Chishtian Science College itself, and education will
            be free for the student.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-subtitle">OTHER DISCOUNTS:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            For Afternoon Programs at CSC, students have another
            opportunity to avail a 50% discount on fees and receive
            quality education without financial constraints. All the
            scholarship categories mentioned above can also be applied
            to students studying in Afternoon classes.
        </p>

        <h3 class="fee-note-title">Note:</h3>
        <p class="fee-description">
            To continue availing the concession or scholarship,
            students must maintain 90% attendance.
        </p>
    </section>

<script>
const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
    const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("show");
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", isOpen ? "Close navigation" : "Open navigation");
    const icon = menuToggle.querySelector("i");
    icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !isOpen);
    icon.classList.toggle("fa-xmark", isOpen);
});

document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const parent = this.parentElement;
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                if (item !== parent) item.classList.remove("open");
            });
            parent.classList.toggle("open");
        }
    });
});

document.querySelectorAll(".navbar > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
            navbar.classList.remove("show");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            menuToggle.querySelector("i").classList.replace("fa-xmark", "fa-bars");
        }
    });
});

window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
    if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
        navbar.classList.remove("show");
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
            item.classList.remove("open");
        });
        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        menuToggle.querySelector("i").classList.replace("fa-xmark", "fa-bars");
    }
});

const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");
window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
    backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

            financial assistance. Eligible students may apply under the
            college's applicable scholarship and concession categories.
            The availability, amount, and conditions of each concession
            should be confirmed with the college administration.
        </p>

                waiver depend on the college's current policy.
            </p>
        </section>

        <section class="fee-item">
            <h3>2. Teachers' Children</h3>
            <p>

                Applicants should confirm the eligibility criteria and
                required documents with the college administration.
            </p>
        </section>

        <section class="fee-item">
            <h3>3. Kinship Concession</h3>
            <p>
                Students who have eligible family members studying at, or
                previously associated with, the institution may ask the
                administration whether a family concession is available.
            </p>
        </section>

        <section class="fee-item">
            <h3>4. Orphan Students</h3>
            <p>

                and the documents needed to apply.
            </p>
        </section>

        <section class="fee-item">
            <h3>5. Students with Special Needs</h3>

                to discuss available support and any applicable fee
                concession arrangements.
            </p>
        </section>

        <section class="fee-item">

            </p>
        </section>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const form = document.getElementById("applicationForm");
    const message = document.getElementById("successMessage");

    if (form && message) {
        form.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            if (!form.reportValidity()) {
                return;
            }

            message.style.display = "block";
            message.scrollIntoView({
                behavior: "smooth",
                block: "center"
            });
        });
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
    const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("show");
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", isOpen ? "Close navigation" : "Open navigation");
    const icon = menuToggle.querySelector("i");
    icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !isOpen);
    icon.classList.toggle("fa-xmark", isOpen);
});

document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const parent = this.parentElement;
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                if (item !== parent) item.classList.remove("open");
            });
            parent.classList.toggle("open");
        }
    });
});

document.querySelectorAll(".navbar > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
            navbar.classList.remove("show");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            menuToggle.querySelector("i").classList.replace("fa-xmark", "fa-bars");
        }
    });
});

window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
    if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
        navbar.classList.remove("show");
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
            item.classList.remove("open");
        });
        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        menuToggle.querySelector("i").classList.replace("fa-xmark", "fa-bars");
    }
});

const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");
window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
    backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
