<section class="history-about">
    <p class="history-heading">
        Chishtian Science Colleges has a proud legacy of academic excellence
        and student achievement. The institution began with a vision to
        provide quality education and continues to empower students across Pakistan.
    </p>

    <p class="history-text">
        Established as a leading institution for science education, Chishtian
        Science College has built a strong reputation for academic excellence
        in intermediate examinations. The college is widely recognized for its
        outstanding performance, particularly in the Bahawalpur division, where
        its students consistently achieve top positions. Catering specifically
        to science students, the institution prepares learners for higher
        education in fields such as engineering and medicine. Over the years,
        it has emerged as a focused and high-achieving private sector college.
        In November 2025, the college was highlighted for securing 43 board
        positions, further strengthening its status as a prominent and successful
        institution in the region. It is important to note that this refers to
        the private sector Chishtian Science College, distinct from the Government
        Graduate College Chishtian established in 1962.
    </p>
</section>

{% endblock %}

<script>
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menuToggle");
    const navLinks = document.getElementById("navLinks");

    menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle("active");
        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
        menuToggle.setAttribute(
            "aria-label",
            isOpen ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu"
        );
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown-button").forEach(function (button) {
        button.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const dropdown = button.closest(".dropdown");
            const willOpen = !dropdown.classList.contains("open");

            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });

            if (willOpen) dropdown.classList.add("open");
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (!event.target.closest(".dropdown")) {
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });
        }
    });

    const backToTop = document.getElementById("backToTop");

    window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
        backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 250 ? "block" : "none";
    });

    backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    });
</script>

</body>
</html>

<div class="history-page">

    <section class="history-hero">
        <div>
            <h1>BACKGROUND RIPAH</h1>
            <hr>
        </div>
    </section>

    <main class="history-content">
        <div class="history-container">

            <p class="history-heading">
                Riphah International College (RIC) Chishtian is an educational
                institution focused on academic learning, skills development,
                and students' personal growth.
            </p>

            <div class="history-text">

                <p>
                    Riphah International College (RIC) Chishtian is located
                    on 13-G Road, near Daranwala Mor, Chishtian. It is part
                    of the Riphah educational network in Pakistan and aims
                    to provide students with educational opportunities and
                    a supportive learning environment.
                </p>

                <p>
                    The college offers intermediate programs such as FSc
                    Pre-Medical, FSc Pre-Engineering, ICS, FA/FA IT, and
                    I.Com. It also offers Associate Degree Programs in
                    different fields. Students should contact the college
                    to confirm the programs available for the current
                    academic session.
                </p>

                <p>
                    The institution aims to combine academic education
                    with modern skills. Depending on the available programs
                    and activities, students may have opportunities to
                    explore technology, freelancing, e-commerce, and
                    digital marketing.
                </p>

                <p>
                    Riphah College Chishtian also aims to provide a learning
                    environment that supports students' academic and
                    personal development. Computer facilities and
                    extracurricular activities can help students develop
                    practical skills, confidence, and teamwork.
                </p>

                <p>
                    The wider Riphah network emphasizes education alongside
                    ethical values and character development. Students are
                    encouraged to pursue academic progress while developing
                    responsibility, respect, and positive social values.
                </p>

            </div>

            <section class="history-highlight">
                <h2>Our Aim</h2>
                <p>
                    To support students through quality education, practical
                    learning opportunities, and personal development so they
                    can prepare for future academic and professional goals.
                </p>
            </section>

        </div>
    </main>

</div>
{% endblock %}

                <img src="{% static 'images/dirsup.PNG' %}"
                     alt="Sir Asif Iqbal, Director"
                     loading="lazy">
            </div>

            <div class="history-text">
                <h3>Sir Asif Iqbal, Director</h3>

                <p>
                    Under the inspiring leadership of Sir Asif Iqbal,
                    our institution is committed to academic excellence,
                    character building, and holistic student development.
                </p>

                <p>
                    Superior College Chishtian, part of the extensive
                    Superior Group of Colleges founded by Prof. Dr. Ch.
                    Abdul Rehman in 2000, is a prominent educational
                    institute located on Bahawalnagar Road, Chishtian.
                    Superior College Chishtian is located at 2-km
                    Bahawalnagar Road, Chishtian Mandi, Punjab, Pakistan.
                    Superior Group of Colleges Chishtian offers
                    Intermediate programs including FSc, ICS, FA along
                    with professional diplomas.
                </p>

                <p>
                    Particularly for girls, this college is an important
                    educational opportunity because many degree programs
                    match students' educational aspirations and future
                    goals. The original page describes 2019 as a planned
                    period of growth for Superior, including additional
                    departments, degree programs, faculty and students.
                </p>

                <p>
                    The campus provides scholarship opportunities,
                    transport facilities, and academic support.
                </p>
            </div>

        </div>
    </article>

</div>
{% endblock %}

    <!-- =====================================================
         JAVASCRIPT
    ====================================================== -->

    <script>

        /* =========================================
           MOBILE DROPDOWNS
        ========================================= */

        const dropdowns =
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown");

        dropdowns.forEach(function (dropdown) {

            const dropdownToggle =
                dropdown.querySelector(".dropdown-toggle");

            dropdownToggle.addEventListener("click", function (event) {

                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {

                    event.preventDefault();

                    dropdown.classList.toggle("active");

                }

            });

        });


        /* =========================================
           BACK TO TOP
        ========================================= */

        const backToTop =
            document.getElementById("back-to-top");


        window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {

            if (window.scrollY > 200) {

                backToTop.style.display = "block";

            } else {

                backToTop.style.display = "none";

            }

        });


        /* =========================================
           CARD HOVER
        ========================================= */

        const cards =
            document.querySelectorAll(".college-card");

        cards.forEach(function (card) {

            card.addEventListener("mouseenter", function () {

                card.classList.add("card-active");

            });

            card.addEventListener("mouseleave", function () {

                card.classList.remove("card-active");

            });

        });

    </script>

</body>

</html>

<script>
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menuToggle");
    const navLinks = document.getElementById("navLinks");

    menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const isOpen = navLinks.classList.toggle("active");
        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen);
        menuToggle.setAttribute(
            "aria-label",
            isOpen ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu"
        );
    });

    document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown-button").forEach(function (button) {
        button.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const dropdown = button.closest(".dropdown");
            const willOpen = !dropdown.classList.contains("open");

            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });

            if (willOpen) dropdown.classList.add("open");
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (!event.target.closest(".dropdown")) {
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });
        }
    });

    const backToTop = document.getElementById("backToTop");

    window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
        backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 250 ? "block" : "none";
    });

    backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    });
</script>

</body>

    <script>

        /* ================= IMAGE SLIDER ================= */

        let slides =
            document.querySelectorAll(".slide");

        let index = 0;


        function showSlides() {

            slides.forEach(function (slide) {

                slide.classList.remove("active");

            });

            index++;

            if (index >= slides.length) {

                index = 0;

            }

            slides[index].classList.add("active");

        }


        setInterval(showSlides, 3000);


        /* ================= REVEAL ANIMATION ================= */

        function revealCards() {

            const reveals =
                document.querySelectorAll(".reveal");

            reveals.forEach(function (element) {

                const windowHeight =
                    window.innerHeight;

                const elementTop =
                    element.getBoundingClientRect().top;

                const visible = 150;


                if (elementTop <
                    windowHeight - visible) {

                    element.classList.add("active");

                }

            });

        }


        window.addEventListener(
            "scroll",
            revealCards
        );

        revealCards();

/* ================= COUNTERS ================= */

const counters = document.querySelectorAll(".counter");
const statsSection = document.querySelector(".stats-section");

let countersStarted = false;

function startCounters() {

    // Stop if this page has no stats section.
    if (!statsSection || countersStarted) {
        return;
    }

    const sectionTop = statsSection.getBoundingClientRect().top;

    if (sectionTop < window.innerHeight - 100) {

        countersStarted = true;

        counters.forEach(function (counter) {

            const target = Number(
                counter.getAttribute("data-target")
            );

            let current = 0;

            const increment = Math.max(
                1,
                Math.ceil(target / 100)
            );

            function updateCounter() {

                current += increment;

                if (current >= target) {
                    counter.innerText = target;
                    return;
                }

                counter.innerText = current;

                setTimeout(updateCounter, 20);
            }

            updateCounter();

        });
    }
}

window.addEventListener("scroll", startCounters);
startCounters();
/* ================= MORE NEWS ================= */

const moreBtn = document.getElementById("moreBtn");

const hiddenEvents = document.querySelectorAll(".hidden");

if (moreBtn) {
    moreBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {

        hiddenEvents.forEach(function (event) {
            event.style.display = "block";
        });

        moreBtn.style.display = "none";

    });
}

    </script>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
            const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");
            const dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll(".navbar .dropdown");
            const dropdownLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".navbar .dropdown-toggle");
            const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");

            // Open and close the mobile navigation.
            menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
                const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
                menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
                menuToggle.setAttribute(
                    "aria-label",
                    isOpen ? "Close navigation menu" : "Open navigation menu"
                );

                const icon = menuToggle.querySelector("i");
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-bars", !isOpen);
                icon.classList.toggle("fa-xmark", isOpen);
            });

            // Dropdowns: click on mobile; normal hover/focus on desktop.
            dropdownLinks.forEach(function (link) {
                link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                        event.preventDefault();

                        const dropdown = link.closest(".dropdown");
                        const shouldOpen = !dropdown.classList.contains("open");

                        dropdowns.forEach(function (item) {
                            item.classList.remove("open");
                            const itemLink = item.querySelector(".dropdown-toggle");
                            if (itemLink) {
                                itemLink.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                            }
                        });

                        if (shouldOpen) {
                            dropdown.classList.add("open");
                            link.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
                        }
                    } else {
                        event.preventDefault();
                    }
                });
            });

            // Close mobile navigation after selecting a real page link.
            navbar.querySelectorAll("a:not(.dropdown-toggle)").forEach(function (link) {
                link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                        navbar.classList.remove("active");
                        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-label", "Open navigation menu");

                        const icon = menuToggle.querySelector("i");
                        icon.classList.add("fa-bars");
                        icon.classList.remove("fa-xmark");
                    }
                });
            });

            // Reset mobile-only state when returning to desktop width.
            window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
                if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("active");
                    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

                    dropdowns.forEach(function (item) {
                        item.classList.remove("open");
                        const link = item.querySelector(".dropdown-toggle");
                        if (link) {
                            link.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                        }
                    });

                    const icon = menuToggle.querySelector("i");
                    icon.classList.add("fa-bars");
                    icon.classList.remove("fa-xmark");
                }
            });

            // Show the back-to-top button after scrolling.
            function updateBackToTop() {
                backToTop.style.display =
                    window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
            }

            window.addEventListener("scroll", updateBackToTop);
            updateBackToTop();

            backToTop.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
                event.preventDefault();
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
            });
        });
    </script>

    {% block extra_js %}{% endblock extra_js %}
</body>
</html>

<script>
(function () {
    const button = document.getElementById("pgc-back-to-top");

    if (!button) return;

    function updateButton() {
        button.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
    }

    window.addEventListener("scroll", updateButton);
    updateButton();

    button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    const menuToggle = document.getElementById("pgc-menu-toggle");
    const navbar = document.getElementById("pgc-navbar");

    if (!menuToggle || !navbar) return;

    menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
        menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
        menuToggle.innerHTML = isOpen
            ? '<i class="fas fa-times"></i>'
            : '<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>';
    });

    navbar.querySelectorAll(".pgc-dropdown-toggle").forEach(function (link) {
        link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                event.preventDefault();

                const dropdown = this.parentElement;

                navbar.querySelectorAll(".pgc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                    if (item !== dropdown) item.classList.remove("open");
                });

                dropdown.classList.toggle("open");
            }
        });
    });

    navbar.querySelectorAll("a:not(.pgc-dropdown-toggle), .pgc-sub-menu a")
        .forEach(function (link) {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 767) {
                    navbar.classList.remove("active");
                    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                    menuToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>';
                }
            });
        });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
            navbar.classList.remove("active");
            navbar.querySelectorAll(".pgc-dropdown").forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("open");
            });
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            menuToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>';
        }
    });
})();
</script>

<script>

const pictureInput =
    document.getElementById('profile_picture');

const fileName =
    document.getElementById('file-name');


if (pictureInput) {

    pictureInput.addEventListener(
        'change',
        function () {

            if (this.files.length > 0) {

                fileName.textContent =
                    this.files[0].name;

            } else {

                fileName.textContent =
                    'No picture selected';

            }

        }
    );

}

</script>

<!-- =========================
     JAVASCRIPT
========================= -->

<script>

    /* =========================
       AUTO IMAGE SLIDER
    ========================= */

    const slides =
        document.querySelectorAll(".slide");

    let index = 0;


    function showSlides() {

        slides.forEach(function (slide) {

            slide.classList.remove("active");

        });


        index++;


        if (index >= slides.length) {

            index = 0;

        }


        slides[index].classList.add("active");

    }


    setInterval(showSlides, 3000);


</script>

</a>

<script>
(function () {
    const button = document.getElementById("riphah-back-to-top");
    if (!button || button.dataset.initialized === "true") return;
    button.dataset.initialized = "true";

    function updateButton() {
        button.classList.toggle(
            "riphah-back-to-top-visible",
            window.scrollY > 200
        );
    }

    window.addEventListener("scroll", updateButton, { passive: true });
    updateButton();

    button.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        window.scrollTo({
            top: 0,
            behavior: "smooth"
        });
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    const header = document.querySelector(".riphah-header");
    if (!header || header.dataset.menuInitialized === "true") return;
    header.dataset.menuInitialized = "true";

    const toggle = header.querySelector("#riphah-menu-toggle");
    const navbar = header.querySelector("#riphah-navbar");
    const dropdowns = header.querySelectorAll(".riphah-dropdown");

    if (!toggle || !navbar) return;

    function closeMenu() {
        navbar.classList.remove("riphah-active");
        toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

        dropdowns.forEach(function (dropdown) {
            dropdown.classList.remove("riphah-open");
        });
    }

    toggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const isOpen = navbar.classList.toggle("riphah-active");
        toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
    });

    dropdowns.forEach(function (dropdown) {
        const link = dropdown.querySelector(".riphah-dropdown-toggle");
        if (!link) return;

        link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            if (window.innerWidth > 767 || link.getAttribute("href") !== "#") {
                return;
            }

            event.preventDefault();

            const wasOpen = dropdown.classList.contains("riphah-open");

            dropdowns.forEach(function (item) {
                item.classList.remove("riphah-open");
            });

            if (!wasOpen) {
                dropdown.classList.add("riphah-open");
            }
        });
    });

    navbar.querySelectorAll("a").forEach(function (link) {
        link.addEventListener("click", function () {
            if (
                window.innerWidth <= 767 &&
                link.getAttribute("href") !== "#"
            ) {
                closeMenu();
            }
        });
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
            closeMenu();
        }
    });
})();
</script>

<!-- =====================================================
     JAVASCRIPT
===================================================== -->

<script>


/* =====================================================
   MOBILE NAV
===================================================== */

const menuToggle =
    document.getElementById("menu-toggle");

const navbar =
    document.getElementById("navbar");


menuToggle.addEventListener("click",()=>{

    navbar.classList.toggle("active");

    menuToggle.innerHTML =
        navbar.classList.contains("active")

        ? '<i class="fas fa-times"></i>'

        : '<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>';

});


document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a")
.forEach(item=>{

    item.addEventListener("click",e=>{

        if(window.innerWidth<=768){

            e.preventDefault();

            const menu =
                item.parentElement
                    .querySelector(".sub-menu");


            document
            .querySelectorAll(".sub-menu")
            .forEach(m=>{

                if(m!==menu){

                    m.style.display="none";

                }

            });


            menu.style.display =
                menu.style.display==="block"
                ? "none"
                : "block";

        }

    });

});


document.querySelectorAll(".navbar a")
.forEach(link=>{

    link.addEventListener("click",()=>{

        if(
            window.innerWidth<=768 &&
            !link.parentElement
            .classList.contains("dropdown")
        ){

            navbar.classList.remove("active");

            menuToggle.innerHTML =
                '<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>';

        }

    });

});


/* =====================================================
   HERO SLIDER
===================================================== */

const slides =
    document.querySelectorAll(".slide");

let slideIndex=0;


setInterval(()=>{

    slides[slideIndex]
        .classList.remove("active");


    slideIndex =
        (slideIndex+1) %
        slides.length;


    slides[slideIndex]
        .classList.add("active");

},4000);


/* =====================================================
   STATS COUNT UP
===================================================== */

let statsDone=false;


function animateStats(){

    const section =
        document.querySelector(".stats-section");


    if(!section || statsDone){
        return;
    }


    if(
        section.getBoundingClientRect().top
        <
        window.innerHeight*.82
    ){

        statsDone=true;


        document
        .querySelectorAll(".stat-box")
        .forEach((box,i)=>{


            setTimeout(()=>{

                box.classList.add("show");

            },i*100);


            const number =
                box.querySelector("h2");


            const target =
                Number(number.dataset.target);


            let start=0;

            const duration=900;

            const begin =
                performance.now();


            function count(now){

                const progress =
                    Math.min(
                        (now-begin)/duration,
                        1
                    );


                number.textContent =
                    Math.floor(
                        progress*target
                    ).toLocaleString();


                if(progress<1){

                    requestAnimationFrame(count);

                }

            }


            requestAnimationFrame(count);

        });

    }

}


window.addEventListener(
    "scroll",
    animateStats
);


window.addEventListener(
    "load",
    animateStats
);


/* =====================================================
   PROGRAM SEARCH
===================================================== */

const programCards =
    [
        ...document.querySelectorAll(".program-card")
    ];


const allProgramNames = [

    "Engineering and Technology",

    "B.Sc Computer Science",

    "BS IT",

    "B.Sc Mathematics",

    "ADP-CS",

    "Business and Management Sciences",

    "BS Entrepreneurship",

    "ADP & BS English",

    "ADP & BS Marketing",

    "B.A.D",

    "Medical Sciences",

    "ADP & BS Chemistry",

    "ADP & BS Botany",

    "ADP & BS Nursing",

    "ADP & BS Zoology"

];


const input =
    document.getElementById(
        "searchInput"
    );


const suggestions =
    document.getElementById(
        "suggestions"
    );


const searchResult =
    document.getElementById(
        "searchResult"
    );


function normalize(s){

    return s
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(
            /[^a-z0-9]+/g,
            " "
        )
        .trim();

}


function clearProgramHighlights(){

    programCards
    .forEach(c=>{

        c.classList.remove(
            "highlight"
        );

    });


    document
    .querySelectorAll(".sub-box")
    .forEach(x=>{

        x.classList.remove(
            "match-highlight"
        );

    });

}


/* INPUT */

input.addEventListener(
    "input",
    ()=>{

        const q =
            normalize(
                input.value
            );


        suggestions.innerHTML="";

        searchResult.textContent="";

        clearProgramHighlights();


        if(!q){

            return;

        }


        const found =
            allProgramNames
            .filter(
                n =>
                normalize(n)
                .includes(q)
            )
            .slice(0,8);


        if(!found.length){

            const li =
                document.createElement(
                    "li"
                );


            li.textContent =
                "No Program Found";


            li.style.cursor =
                "default";


            suggestions
                .appendChild(li);


            return;

        }


        found.forEach(name=>{

            const li =
                document.createElement(
                    "li"
                );


            li.textContent=name;


            li.addEventListener(
                "click",
                ()=>{

                    input.value=name;

                    suggestions
                        .innerHTML="";

                    performSearch();

                }
            );


            suggestions
                .appendChild(li);

        });

    }
);


/* SEARCH FUNCTION */

function performSearch(){

    const q =
        normalize(
            input.value
        );


    suggestions.innerHTML="";

    clearProgramHighlights();


    if(!q){

        searchResult.textContent =
            "Please enter a program name.";

        return;

    }


    let matches=[];


    programCards.forEach(card=>{


        const cardName =
            normalize(
                card.dataset.name
            );


        if(cardName.includes(q)){

            card.classList.add(
                "highlight"
            );

            matches.push(card);

        }


        card
        .querySelectorAll(
            ".program-name,.sub-box"
        )
        .forEach(item=>{


            if(
                normalize(
                    item.textContent
                ).includes(q)
            ){

                if(
                    item.classList
                    .contains("sub-box")
                ){

                    item.classList.add(
                        "match-highlight"
                    );

                }


                card.classList.add(
                    "highlight"
                );


                matches.push(item);

            }

        });

    });


    if(!matches.length){

        searchResult.textContent =
            "No Program Found â€” please try another program name.";

        return;

    }


    const target =
        matches[0];


    target.scrollIntoView({

        behavior:"smooth",

        block:"center"

    });


    searchResult.textContent =
        "Program found â€” highlighted below.";


    setTimeout(()=>{

        searchResult.textContent="";

    },3000);

}


document
.getElementById("searchBtn")
.addEventListener(
    "click",
    performSearch
);


document.addEventListener(
    "click",
    e=>{

        if(
            !input.contains(e.target) &&
            !suggestions.contains(e.target)
        ){

            suggestions.innerHTML="";

        }

    }
);


/* =====================================================
   MORE EVENTS
===================================================== */

document
.getElementById("moreBtn")
.addEventListener(
    "click",
    ()=>{


        document
        .querySelectorAll(
            ".event-card.hidden"
        )
        .forEach(c=>{

            c.classList.remove(
                "hidden"
            );

        });


        document
        .getElementById("moreBtn")
        .style.display="none";

    }
);


/* =====================================================
   FACULTY SLIDER
===================================================== */

const facultyContainer =
    document.querySelector(
        ".testimonials-container"
    );


const facultyCards =
    [
        ...document.querySelectorAll(
            ".testimonial-card"
        )
    ];


let facultyIndex=0;


function facultyPerPage(){

    if(window.innerWidth<=768){

        return 1;

    }

    if(window.innerWidth<=1100){

        return 2;

    }

    return 3;

}


function updateFaculty(){

    if(!facultyCards.length){

        return;

    }


    const per =
        facultyPerPage();


    const gap=15;


    const width =
        facultyCards[0]
        .getBoundingClientRect()
        .width;


    const max =
        Math.max(
            0,
            facultyCards.length-per
        );


    facultyIndex =
        Math.min(
            facultyIndex,
            max
        );


    facultyContainer.style.transform =
        `translateX(-${
            facultyIndex*(width+gap)
        }px)`;

}


document
.getElementById("nextBtn")
.addEventListener(
    "click",
    ()=>{

        facultyIndex +=
            facultyPerPage();

        updateFaculty();

    }
);


document
.getElementById("prevBtn")
.addEventListener(
    "click",
    ()=>{

        facultyIndex -=
            facultyPerPage();


        if(facultyIndex<0){

            facultyIndex=0;

        }


        updateFaculty();

    }
);


window.addEventListener(
    "resize",
    updateFaculty
);


window.addEventListener(
    "load",
    updateFaculty
);


/* =====================================================
   SCHOLARSHIP CALCULATOR
===================================================== */

document
.getElementById(
    "checkEligibility"
)
.addEventListener(
    "click",
    ()=>{


        const pctInput =
            document.getElementById(
                "percentageInput"
            );


        const marksInput =
            document.getElementById(
                "marksInput"
            );


        const totalInput =
            document.getElementById(
                "totalMarksInput"
            );


        const result =
            document.getElementById(
                "calcResult"
            );


        let percentage=null;


        const typedPct =
            parseFloat(
                pctInput.value
            );


        const marks =
            parseFloat(
                marksInput.value
            );


        const total =
            parseFloat(
                totalInput.value
            );


        /* DIRECT PERCENTAGE */

        if(!Number.isNaN(typedPct)){

            percentage=typedPct;

        }


        /* MARKS */

        else if(
            !Number.isNaN(marks) &&
            !Number.isNaN(total) &&
            total>0
        ){

            percentage =
                (marks/total)*100;


            pctInput.value =
                percentage.toFixed(2);

        }


        /* EMPTY */

        else{

            result.className =
                "calc-result not-eligible";


            result.textContent =
                "Please enter a percentage, or enter obtained and total marks.";

            return;

        }


        /* INVALID */

        if(
            percentage<0 ||
            percentage>100
        ){

            result.className =
                "calc-result not-eligible";


            result.textContent =
                "Please enter a percentage between 0% and 100%.";

            return;

        }


        /* 80% OR ABOVE */

        result.className =
            percentage>=80

            ? "calc-result eligible"

            : "calc-result not-eligible";


        result.textContent =
            percentage>=80

            ? `Congratulations! You are eligible for the merit scholarship at ${percentage.toFixed(2)}%.`

            : `Your percentage is ${percentage.toFixed(2)}%. You are not eligible for the 80% merit-scholarship threshold.`;

    }
);


/* =====================================================
   SHARE BUTTON
===================================================== */


/* =====================================================
   SHARE BUTTON
===================================================== */

const shareBtn = document.getElementById("shareBtn");

if (shareBtn) {
    shareBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {

        const data = {
            title: document.title,
            text: "Superior College Chishtian",
            url: window.location.href
        };

        try {
            if (navigator.share) {
                await navigator.share(data);
            }

            else if (navigator.clipboard) {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(
                    window.location.href
                );

                alert(
                    "Page link copied. You can now share it anywhere."
                );
            }

            else {
                prompt(
                    "Copy this page link:",
                    window.location.href
                );
            }
        }

        catch (err) {
            // User cancelled sharing.
        }

    });
}


/* =====================================================
   BACK TO TOP
===================================================== */

const topBtn =
    document.getElementById(
        "back-to-top"
    );


window.addEventListener(
    "scroll",
    ()=>{

        topBtn.style.display =
            window.scrollY>400
            ? "flex"
            : "none";

    }
);

</script>

</body>
</html>

<button id="back-to-top" aria-label="Back to top"><i class="fas fa-chevron-up"></i></button>

<script>
const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
    const opened = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", opened);
});

document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 1050) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const item = this.parentElement;
            const opening = !item.classList.contains("open");
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(d => d.classList.remove("open"));
            if (opening) item.classList.add("open");
        }
    });
});

document.querySelectorAll(".degree-header").forEach(function (heading) {
    function toggle() {
        const item = heading.closest(".degree-item");
        const opening = !item.classList.contains("open");
        document.querySelectorAll(".degree-item").forEach(function (other) {
            other.classList.remove("open");
            other.querySelector(".degree-header").setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        });
        if (opening) {
            item.classList.add("open");
            heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
        }
    }
    heading.addEventListener("click", toggle);
    heading.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
        if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") {
            event.preventDefault();
            toggle();
        }
    });
});

const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");
window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
    backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
});
backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
    window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: "smooth"});
});

window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
    if (window.innerWidth > 1050) {
        navbar.classList.remove("active");
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(d => d.classList.remove("open"));
    }
});
</script>

<script>
const menuToggle = document.getElementById("menu-toggle");
const navbar = document.getElementById("navbar");

menuToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
    const opened = navbar.classList.toggle("active");
    menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", opened);
});

document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown > a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 1100) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const dropdown = this.parentElement;
            const opening = !dropdown.classList.contains("open");
            document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(d => d.classList.remove("open"));
            if (opening) dropdown.classList.add("open");
        }
    });
});

document.querySelectorAll(".navbar > a, .sub-menu a").forEach(function (link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 1100) {
            navbar.classList.remove("active");
            menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        }
    });
});

const backToTop = document.getElementById("back-to-top");
window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
    backToTop.style.display = window.scrollY > 200 ? "flex" : "none";
});
backToTop.addEventListener("click", function () {
    window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: "smooth"});
});

window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
    if (window.innerWidth > 1100) {
        navbar.classList.remove("active");
        document.querySelectorAll(".dropdown").forEach(d => d.classList.remove("open"));
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
